def test_schema_integrity():
    """Compara schema.sql con la BD real."""
    print(color("\n🔬 Verificando integridad schema...", "1;33"))
    err = 0
    try:
        if not Path("schema.sql").exists():
            err += fail("schema.sql no encontrado")
            return err
        with open("schema.sql") as f:
            schema_sql = f.read()
        # Contar tablas en schema
        import re
        schema_tables = re.findall(r'CREATE TABLE(?: IF NOT EXISTS)? (\w+)', schema_sql)
        conn = sqlite3.connect("tutte_game.db")
        cur = conn.cursor()
        cur.execute("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name NOT LIKE 'sqlite_%'")
        db_tables = [r[0] for r in cur.fetchall()]
        conn.close()
        faltan = set(schema_tables) - set(db_tables)
        sobran = set(db_tables) - set(schema_tables)
        if not faltan and not sobran:
            ok(f"Coherencia perfecta ({len(schema_tables)} tablas)")
        else:
            if faltan:
                err += fail(f"Tablas en schema pero no en BD: {faltan}")
            if sobran:
                info(f"Tablas en BD pero no en schema: {sobran}")
    except Exception as e:
        err += fail(f"Error de integridad: {e}")
    return err


def test_banco_journaling():
    """Verifica el sistema de journaling del banco."""
    print(color("\n🏦 Probando journaling bancario...", "1;33"))
    err = 0
    try:
        import sqlite3
        conn = sqlite3.connect("tutte_game.db")
        # Crear tabla si no existe
        conn.execute("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS banco_ledger (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, user_id INTEGER NOT NULL, monto INTEGER NOT NULL, concepto TEXT, origen TEXT, fecha TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES jugadores(user_id))")
        
        uid_a, uid_b = 1001, 1002
        monto = 500
        
        # Insertar entradas de prueba
        conn.execute("INSERT INTO banco_ledger (user_id, monto, concepto, origen) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)", 
                     (uid_a, -monto, "Transferencia a B", str(uid_b)))
        conn.execute("INSERT INTO banco_ledger (user_id, monto, concepto, origen) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)", 
                     (uid_b, monto, "Transferencia de A", str(uid_a)))
        conn.commit()
        
        # Verificar saldos
        saldo_a = conn.execute("SELECT COALESCE(SUM(monto), 0) FROM banco_ledger WHERE user_id = ?", (uid_a,)).fetchone()[0]
        saldo_b = conn.execute("SELECT COALESCE(SUM(monto), 0) FROM banco_ledger WHERE user_id = ?", (uid_b,)).fetchone()[0]
        
        if saldo_a == -monto and saldo_b == monto:
            ok(f"Journaling correcto: A={saldo_a}, B={saldo_b}")
        else:
            err += fail(f"Journaling incorrecto: A={saldo_a}, B={saldo_b}")
        
        # Limpiar datos de prueba
        conn.execute("DELETE FROM banco_ledger WHERE user_id IN (?, ?)", (uid_a, uid_b))
        conn.commit()
        conn.close()
    except Exception as e:
        err += fail(f"Error en test de journaling: {e}")
    return err


def main():
    print("╔═══════════════════════════════════════╗")
    print("║   ITSŲKI MAINFRAṀE · VERIFICACIÓN    ║")
    print("╚═══════════════════════════════════════╝")
    total_errors = 0
    total_errors += test_imports()
    total_errors += test_database()
    total_errors += test_schema_integrity()
    total_errors += test_idiomas()
    total_errors += test_itsunario()
    total_errors += test_minigames_structure()
    total_errors += test_montecarlo_fichas()
    total_errors += test_banco_journaling()

    print("\n" + "="*30)
    if total_errors == 0:
        print(color("🎉 Todas las pruebas pasaron sin errores", "1;32"))
    else:
        print(color(f"⚠ {total_errors} errores encontrados", "1;33"))
    print("="*30)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

══════════ code_audit.py ══════════
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
ITSŲKI MAINFRAṀE AUDIT · v2.0 · DETECCIÓN REAL
Solo marca cadenas que son argumentos literales de funciones de salida.
Ignora claves de diccionario, f‑strings, formatos de log y URLs.
"""
import os, re, ast
from pathlib import Path
from collections import defaultdict

PROJECT_ROOT = Path.cwd()
REPORT_FILE = PROJECT_ROOT / "audit_report.log"

COMMANDS_DIR = "commands"

IGNORE_DIRS = {'__pycache__', '.git', 'itsuki-web', 'backups', 'logs', 'tools', 'itsuki-bot'}
EXCLUDE_FILES = {'code_audit.py', 'bot.py', 'activar_sizania.py', 'mainframe_validator.py',
                 'itsuki.py', 'main.py'}

PYTHON_EXT = '.py'
I18N_FUNCTIONS = {'obtener_texto', 'formatear_texto'}
SALIDA_FUNCS = {'safe_reply', 'send_message', 'send_photo', 'reply_to',
                'answer_callback_query', 'edit_message_text', 'edit_message_caption',
                'send_audio', 'send_video', 'send_document', 'send_voice',
                'msg_queue.put'}

HTML_TAG_RE = re.compile(r'<[a-zA-Z/][^>]*>')
URL_RE = re.compile(r'https?://\S+')
SQL_REAL_RE = re.compile(r'\b(SELECT\s+.*\bFROM|INSERT\s+INTO|UPDATE\s+.*\bSET|DELETE\s+FROM)\b', re.IGNORECASE)
STRUCTURAL_CHARS_RE = re.compile(r'[─┌┐└┘│╭╰⊛⟐⟪⟫✍╔╗╚╝║═]')

issues = {
    "hardcoded_strings": [],
    "missing_formatters": [],
    "html_in_strings": [],
    "commands_outside_commands": [],
    "duplicate_image_calls": [],
    "start_user_validation": [],
    "schema": [],
    "structure": [],
    "sql_outside_db": [],
    "visual_outside_decorators": [],
}
hardcoded_by_file = defaultdict(int)

statistics = {
    "total_files": 0, "total_py_files": 0, "total_lines": 0,
    "classes": 0, "functions": 0, "decorators": 0,
    "i18n_calls": 0, "formatter_calls": 0,
}

def color(text, code): return f"\033[{code}m{text}\033[0m"
def bold(s): return color(s, "1;37")
def yellow(s): return color(s, "1;33")
def green(s): return color(s, "1;32")
def cyan(s): return color(s, "1;36")

def inside_fstring(node):
    parent = getattr(node, 'parent', None)
    while parent is not None:
        if isinstance(parent, (ast.JoinedStr, ast.FormattedValue)):
            return True
        parent = getattr(parent, 'parent', None)
    return False

def is_argument_of_output(node):
    """Determina si el nodo Constant es un argumento directo de una función de salida."""
    parent = getattr(node, 'parent', None)
    if parent is None:
        return False
    if isinstance(parent, ast.Call):
        func = parent.func
        if isinstance(func, ast.Attribute) and func.attr in SALIDA_FUNCS:
            return True
        if isinstance(func, ast.Name) and func.id in SALIDA_FUNCS:
            return True
    return False

def add_parents(tree):
    for node in ast.walk(tree):
        for child in ast.iter_child_nodes(node):
            child.parent = node

def analyze_python_file(filepath):
    rel = filepath.relative_to(PROJECT_ROOT)
    if 'tools/' in str(rel):
        return
    with open(filepath, 'r', errors='ignore') as f:
        content = f.read()
        lines = content.splitlines()
    statistics['total_lines'] += len(lines)
    try:
        tree = ast.parse(content, filename=str(filepath))
    except SyntaxError:
        return
    add_parents(tree)

    for node in ast.walk(tree):
        if isinstance(node, ast.ClassDef):
            statistics['classes'] += 1
        elif isinstance(node, ast.FunctionDef):
            statistics['functions'] += 1
            statistics['decorators'] += len(node.decorator_list)

        if isinstance(node, ast.Constant) and isinstance(node.value, str) and len(node.value.strip()) > 3:
            val = node.value
            lineno = node.lineno
            if inside_fstring(node):
                continue
            if not is_argument_of_output(node):
                continue
            if '{' in val or '}' in val:
                continue
            if URL_RE.search(val):
                continue
            if SQL_REAL_RE.search(val):
                continue
            if val in ('HTML', 'Markdown', 'video', 'audio', 'document', 'voice'):
                continue

            issues["hardcoded_strings"].append((str(rel), lineno, 0, val[:70].replace('\n', ' ')))
            hardcoded_by_file[str(rel)] += 1

        if isinstance(node, ast.Call) and isinstance(node.func, ast.Attribute):
            fname = node.func.attr
            if fname in SALIDA_FUNCS:
                statistics['formatter_calls'] += 1
                if fname in ('send_message', 'send_photo'):
                    if lineno <= len(lines):
                        if 'safe_reply' not in lines[lineno-1]:
                            issues["missing_formatters"].append((str(rel), lineno, 0, f"Llamada directa a {fname} sin safe_reply"))

        if isinstance(node, ast.Constant) and isinstance(node.value, str):
            if HTML_TAG_RE.search(node.value):
                if lineno <= len(lines) and ('parse_mode' in lines[lineno-1].lower() and 'html' in lines[lineno-1].lower()):
                    continue
                issues["html_in_strings"].append((str(rel), lineno, 0, node.value[:70].replace('\n', ' ')))

        if isinstance(node, ast.Call) and isinstance(node.func, ast.Name) and node.func.id in I18N_FUNCTIONS:
            statistics['i18n_calls'] += 1

    if f'/{COMMANDS_DIR}/' not in str(filepath.as_posix()):
        for i, line in enumerate(lines, start=1):
            if re.search(r'@\w+\.message_handler\(commands\s*=\s*\[', line):
                issues["commands_outside_commands"].append((str(rel), i, 0, line.strip()))

def add_parents(tree):
    for node in ast.walk(tree):
        for child in ast.iter_child_nodes(node):
            child.parent = node

def analyze_schema_sql():
    pass

def analyze_structure():
    main_py = PROJECT_ROOT / 'main.py'
    if main_py.exists() and main_py.stat().st_size > 5120:
        issues["structure"].append("main.py supera 5KB.")

def print_header():
    print(bold("\n┌" + "─"*70 + "┐"))
    print(bold("│") + cyan(" ITSŲKI AUDIT · v2.0 · SOLO SALIDA REAL ") + bold("│"))
    print(bold("└" + "─"*70 + "┘\n"))

def print_section(title, lst, icon="⚠️", full=False):
    if not lst: return
    print(yellow(f"\n{icon} {title} ({len(lst)} incidencias)"))
    max_items = len(lst) if full else 10
    for item in lst[:max_items]:
        if isinstance(item, str): print(f"   ▶ {item}")
        else: print(f"   ▶ {item[0]}:{item[1]} → {item[3]}")
    if not full and len(lst) > max_items: print(f"   ... y {len(lst)-max_items} más")

def main():
    print_header()
    for root, dirs, files in os.walk(PROJECT_ROOT):
        dirs[:] = [d for d in dirs if d not in IGNORE_DIRS]
        for file in files:
            if file.endswith(PYTHON_EXT) and file not in EXCLUDE_FILES:
                fp = Path(root) / file
                statistics['total_py_files'] += 1
                analyze_python_file(fp)
    statistics['total_files'] = sum(1 for _ in PROJECT_ROOT.rglob('*') if _.is_file() and not any(p in IGNORE_DIRS for p in _.parts))
    analyze_schema_sql()
    analyze_structure()

    print(green("\n📊 ESTADÍSTICAS GENERALES"))
    for k, v in statistics.items(): print(f"   {k.replace('_',' ')}: {v}")

    if hardcoded_by_file:
        total = sum(hardcoded_by_file.values())
        print(yellow(f"\n⚠️ Cadenas hardcodeadas reales (sin i18n): {total} total"))
        print("   Ranking por archivo:")
        for file, count in sorted(hardcoded_by_file.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True):
            print(f"   ▶ {file}: {count}")
    else:
        print(green("   ✅ No se detectaron cadenas hardcodeadas."))

    print_section("Envíos sin safe_reply", issues["missing_formatters"], "🚫")
    print_section("Comandos fuera de 'commands/'", issues["commands_outside_commands"], "📂")

    total_issues = sum(len(v) for v in issues.values()) + sum(hardcoded_by_file.values())
    print(bold(f"\n🔎 {total_issues} incidencias encontradas en total."))

    with open(REPORT_FILE, 'w') as f:
        f.write("ITSUKI AUDIT REPORT v2.0\n" + "="*60 + "\n")
        f.write(f"Python files analizados: {statistics['total_py_files']}\n")
        if hardcoded_by_file:
            f.write("\n[Cadenas hardcodeadas reales]\n")
            for file, count in sorted(hardcoded_by_file.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True):
                f.write(f"  {file}: {count}\n")
        for k, lst in issues.items():
            if lst:
                f.write(f"\n[{k}]\n")
                for item in lst:
                    if isinstance(item, str): f.write(f"  - {item}\n")
                    else: f.write(f"  {item[0]}:{item[1]} -> {item[3]}\n")
    print(cyan(f"📄 Reporte guardado en {REPORT_FILE}"))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

══════════ db_verifier.py ══════════
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Verifica integridad entre schema.sql, tutte_game.db y core/db.py.
Corrige bugs, sugiere mejoras y genera reporte.
"""
import sqlite3, os, re, hashlib, datetime, json
from pathlib import Path

BASE = Path.cwd()
SCHEMA_FILE = BASE / "schema.sql"
DB_FILE = BASE / "tutte_game.db"
DB_PY = BASE / "core" / "db.py"

def color(text, code): return f"\033[{code}m{text}\033[0m"
def red(s): return color(s, "1;31")
def green(s): return color(s, "1;32")
def yellow(s): return color(s, "1;33")
def cyan(s): return color(s, "1;36")

# ─── 1. Extraer definición de schema.sql ─────────────────
schema_tables = {}
with open(SCHEMA_FILE) as f:
    schema_sql = f.read()

# Parsear CREATE TABLE statements
for match in re.finditer(
    r"CREATE TABLE(?: IF NOT EXISTS)? (\w+)\s*\((.*?)\);",
    schema_sql, re.DOTALL | re.IGNORECASE
):
    table_name = match.group(1)
    body = match.group(2)
    columns = []
    for col_match in re.finditer(r"(\w+)\s+(\w+[^,]*(?:\([^)]*\))?)", body):
        col_name = col_match.group(1)
        col_type = col_match.group(2).strip()
        # Evitar capturar constraint keywords
        if col_name.upper() in ('FOREIGN', 'PRIMARY', 'INDEX', 'UNIQUE', 'CHECK'):
            continue
        columns.append((col_name, col_type))
    schema_tables[table_name] = columns

print(f"{cyan('📄 Schema:')} {len(schema_tables)} tablas definidas")

# ─── 2. Extraer estructura de la base de datos ──────────
if DB_FILE.exists():
    conn = sqlite3.connect(str(DB_FILE))
    cursor = conn.cursor()
    cursor.execute("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name NOT LIKE 'sqlite_%'")
    db_tables = [row[0] for row in cursor.fetchall()]
    db_structure = {}
    for table in db_tables:
        cursor.execute(f"PRAGMA table_info({table})")
        db_structure[table] = [(row[1], row[2]) for row in cursor.fetchall()]
    conn.close()
    print(f"{cyan('🗃️  BD:')} {len(db_tables)} tablas encontradas\n")
else:
    print(red("❌ Base de datos no encontrada"))
    exit(1)

# ─── 3. Comparar schema.sql con la BD ─────────────────
discrepancias = []
for table, cols in schema_tables.items():
    if table not in db_structure:
        discrepancias.append(f"Tabla '{table}' existe en schema.sql pero NO en la BD")
        continue
    db_cols = {name: dtype for name, dtype in db_structure[table]}
    schema_cols = {name: dtype for name, dtype in cols}
    
    # Columnas en schema pero no en BD
    for col, dtype in cols:
        if col not in db_cols:
            discrepancias.append(f"Columna '{table}.{col}' ({dtype}) existe en schema.sql pero NO en la BD")
    
    # Columnas en BD pero no en schema
    for col, dtype in db_structure[table]:
        if col not in schema_cols:
            discrepancias.append(f"Columna '{table}.{col}' ({dtype}) existe en la BD pero NO en schema.sql")

# Tablas en BD que no están en schema
for table in db_tables:
    if table not in schema_tables:
        discrepancias.append(f"Tabla '{table}' existe en la BD pero NO en schema.sql")

if discrepancias:
    print(yellow("⚠️  Discrepancias encontradas:"))
    for d in discrepancias:
        print(f"   - {d}")
else:
    print(green("✅ Coherencia perfecta entre schema.sql y la BD"))

# ─── 4. Verificar core/db.py ──────────────────────────
with open(DB_PY) as f:
    db_py_content = f.read()

# Bug: auto-importación
if re.search(r'from core\.db import get_connection', db_py_content):
    print(red("\n🐛 BUG DETECTADO: core/db.py se importa a sí mismo"))
    print("   Línea 1: 'from core.db import get_connection'")
    # Corregir
    fixed = db_py_content.replace('from core.db import get_connection\n', '')
    with open(DB_PY, 'w') as f:
        f.write(fixed)
    print(green("   ✅ Corregido automáticamente"))

# Verificar que las funciones referencien columnas existentes
all_schema_columns = set()
for table, cols in schema_tables.items():
    for col, _ in cols:
        all_schema_columns.add(f"{table}.{col}")
        all_schema_columns.add(col)  # también sin prefijo

func_errors = []
for match in re.finditer(r"def (\w+)\(.*?\):\s+.*?\"(SELECT.*?)\"", db_py_content, re.DOTALL):
    func_name = match.group(1)
    query = match.group(2)
    # Extraer columnas de la consulta (simple: buscar palabras después de SELECT)
    select_part = re.search(r"SELECT\s+(.*?)\s+FROM", query, re.IGNORECASE)
    if select_part:
        columns_in_query = re.findall(r"\b(\w+)\b", select_part.group(1))
        for col in columns_in_query:
            if col.upper() in ('COUNT', 'SUM', 'MIN', 'MAX', 'AVG', 'DISTINCT', 'AS', 'AND', 'OR'):
                continue
            if col not in all_schema_columns and col != '1':
                func_errors.append(f"Columna '{col}' en función '{func_name}()' no encontrada en el schema")

if func_errors:
    print(red("❌ Errores en funciones:"))
    for e in func_errors:
        print(f"   - {e}")
else:
    print(green("✅ Todas las funciones de db.py referencian columnas válidas"))

# ─── 5. Sugerencias de mejora ─────────────────────────
print(cyan("\n📈 Mejoras recomendadas:"))

# a) Normalizar columnas JSON
json_cols = re.findall(r"(\w+)\s+TEXT.*(?:JSON|json)", schema_sql)
if json_cols:
    print(f"   • Columnas JSON detectadas: {', '.join(json_cols)}")
    print("     Considerar crear tablas de relación (1FN)")

# b) CTEs para consultas complejas
complex_queries = [
    ("obtener_ranking_global_grupo", 
     "WITH grupo_puntos AS (SELECT puntos_grupo FROM control_grupos WHERE chat_id = ?)\nSELECT COUNT(*) FROM control_grupos WHERE estado='ACTIVO' AND puntos_grupo > (SELECT * FROM grupo_puntos)"),
    ("obtener_grupo_aleatorio_activo",
     "WITH bloqueados AS (SELECT chat_id FROM config_bot WHERE clave = 'book_blocked_' || ? AND valor = '1')\nSELECT cg.chat_id FROM control_grupos cg WHERE cg.estado='ACTIVO' AND cg.chat_id NOT IN (SELECT * FROM bloqueados) ORDER BY RANDOM() LIMIT 1")
]
print("   • CTEs sugeridas para mejorar rendimiento:")
for name, cte in complex_queries:
    print(f"     - {name}():")
    for line in cte.split('\n'):
        print(f"       {line}")

# c) Vistas
print("   • Vistas útiles para consultas frecuentes:")
print("     CREATE VIEW v_grupos_activos AS SELECT * FROM control_grupos WHERE estado='ACTIVO'")
print("     CREATE VIEW v_ranking_jugadores AS SELECT nombre, puntos_historicos FROM jugadores ORDER BY puntos_historicos DESC")

# d) Migraciones
migrations_dir = BASE / "migrations"
if not migrations_dir.exists():
    migrations_dir.mkdir()
    print(f"   • Carpeta 'migrations/' creada para versionado de cambios")

# ─── 6. Reporte ──────────────────────────────────────
report = {
    "schema_tables": len(schema_tables),
    "db_tables": len(db_tables),
    "discrepancias": len(discrepancias),
    "func_errors": len(func_errors),
    "json_cols": len(json_cols),
    "timestamp": datetime.datetime.now().isoformat()
}
report_file = BASE / "db_report.json"
with open(report_file, 'w') as f:
    json.dump(report, f, indent=2, default=str)
print(f"\n📄 Reporte guardado en {report_file}")
